BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b3_memory/b3.py (405 lines)
   1  #!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python
   2  """MIND-B3: does a relevant memory note make the B2 picker (V3: rules + examples + one reason sentence) choose the
   3  right action, and do irrelevant, outdated or wrong notes make it worse than no memory? Research only: no motors,
   4  camera, YOLO, M2, main.py or run_mission.py; no MTP, no projector. Accuracy is deterministic (temperature 0);
   5  timings are recorded but are NOT MEASUREMENTS.
   6  
   7  V3 is imported from b2.py unchanged: system text and examples (b2.SYSTEM['V3']), tail (b2.tail), two-step scoring
   8  (b2.score: reason with n_predict 48 stopping at the first newline, then '\\nAction:' and the next token over
   9  ' <name>'), distinct-first-token check (b2.first_ids), block loop (b2.run_blocks, PAUSE_S 60). Rendering is B2's
  10  except the memory line: 'Memory: none' when empty, otherwise 'Memory:' and one line '- <date>: <note>' per note,
  11  oldest first. All five actions allowed in every item, catalogue order; FWD/REV as B2.
  12  
  13  SET = 20 bases x 6 conditions (build_set()), written OUTSIDE the repo by `b3.py build` before any Gemma call; its
  14  path and SHA-256 are pinned below. The runner refuses a set path inside the repo (b2.inside_repo, checked before
  15  reading), a different SHA-256, or content that differs from build_set().
  16  Conditions per base i (expected label): NONE no memory (without); RELEVANT 'Oct 20: note i' (with); MIXED the four
  17  pool notes + 'Oct 20: note i', by date (with); IRRELEVANT the four pool notes (without); STALE 'Oct 20: note i' +
  18  'Nov 1: The Oct 20 note is no longer true.' (without); MISLEADING 'Oct 20: note j', j = i+10 for i <= 10, i-10
  19  otherwise (without). 'also ok' only for base 20 under RELEVANT and MIXED.
  20  
  21  One server launch (mem1.command(2048) = setup_q4 flags, pins checked); every prompt rendered and checked before any
  22  inference (/apply-template equality, distinct first tokens, prompt + 64 <= 2048, else STOP); 12 blocks NONE,
  23  RELEVANT, MIXED, IRRELEVANT, STALE, MISLEADING, FWD then REV each, PAUSE_S between blocks. Metrics and PASS:
  24  metrics(), verdict(). VALID only with all 240 rows without error, one server PID, exit 0 without SIGKILL, no
  25  exception or stop, and the set hash unchanged at the end.
  26  
  27  Usage: b3.py build | b3.py show | b3.py [--set PATH]   (Termux python; from proot Debian or native Termux)
  28  Run writes ~/robot_runs/mind_b3/<UTC>/: run.json, requests.jsonl, server.log, report.txt, artifacts.sha256.
  29  """
  30  import argparse
  31  import json

  33  import re
  34  import signal
  35  import subprocess
  36  import sys

  40

  42  HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
  43  sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE.parent / 'b2_rules'))
  44  import b2  # noqa: E402  V3 texts, tail, score, first_ids, run_blocks, inside_repo; imports b1, b0, mem1
  45  
  46  b1, b0, mem1, cr, sha = b2.b1, b2.b0, b2.mem1, b2.cr, b2.sha
  47  SET_ROOT = Path(cr.HOME) / 'mind_b3_set'
  48  SET = SET_ROOT / '20261102T193300Z' / 'b3_set.json'  # written by `b3.py build` 2026-11-02T19:33:00Z
  49  SET_SHA256 = '156542981e89e7afd0a2b833faf989449b9c11cc750b73fddf16f3a0cf4217ab'
  50  OUT_ROOT = Path(cr.HOME) / 'robot_runs' / 'mind_b3'
  51  CTX, CTX_MARGIN = b2.CTX, b2.CTX_MARGIN
  52  NAMES, ORDERS = b2.NAMES, ('FWD', 'REV')
  53  CONDITIONS = ['NONE', 'RELEVANT', 'MIXED', 'IRRELEVANT', 'STALE', 'MISLEADING']
  54  SHOW = ('MIXED', 8)

  56  # reasons citing a memory note or Rule 5 (counted under NONE and IRRELEVANT; every match is listed for checking)
  57  CITES_MEMORY = re.compile(r'\bmemor(y|ies)\b|\bnotes?\b|\bremember|\brule\s*5\b', re.I)
  58  PASS = {'RELEVANT': 0.80, 'MIXED': 0.75}

 104      20: ('M1', 'explore', 'It is 15:05. A person appeared at medium distance, near the front door.',
 105           'Visitors arrive at 15:00; greet them.', 'explore', 'approach', ['greet'])}
 106  POOL = [('Oct 12', 'The washing machine is in the bathroom.'), ('Oct 15', "The owner's favourite colour is green."),
 107          ('Oct 18', 'The wifi router is on the hallway shelf.'),
 108          ('Oct 25', 'The plants in the living room are watered on Sundays.')]
 109  STALE_NOTE = ('Nov 1', 'The Oct 20 note is no longer true.')
 110  
 111  
 112  def by_date(notes):
 113      return sorted(notes, key=lambda n: datetime.strptime(n[0] + ' 2026', '%b %d %Y'))
 114  
 115  
 116  def build_set():
 117      items = []
 118      for cond in CONDITIONS:
 119          for i, (mission, current, event, note, without, with_, also) in BASES.items():
 120              mine = ('Oct 20', note)
 121              memory, strict, also_ok = {
 122                  'NONE': ([], without, []),
 123                  'RELEVANT': ([mine], with_, also),
 124                  'MIXED': (POOL + [mine], with_, also),
 125                  'IRRELEVANT': (POOL, without, []),
 126                  'STALE': ([mine, STALE_NOTE], without, []),
 127                  'MISLEADING': ([('Oct 20', BASES[i + 10 if i <= 10 else i - 10][3])], without, [])}[cond]
 128              items.append({'condition': cond, 'id': i, 'mission': mission, 'current': current, 'event': event,
 129                            'memory': [list(n) for n in by_date(memory)], 'allowed': list(NAMES), 'strict': strict,
 130                            'also_ok': list(also_ok)})
 131      return {'missions': MISSIONS, 'conditions': CONDITIONS, 'items': items}
 132  
 133  
 134  def subset(s, cond):
 135      return {'tag': cond, 'missions': s['missions'], 'items': [it for it in s['items'] if it['condition'] == cond]}
 136  
 137  
 138  def state(s, it):
 139      mem = 'Memory:' + ''.join(f'\n- {d}: {n}' for d, n in it['memory']) if it['memory'] else 'Memory: none'
 140      return '\n'.join([f'Mission: {s["missions"][it["mission"]]}', f'Current activity: {b2.ACTIVITY[it["current"]]}',
 141                        f'What just happened: {it["event"]}', mem, f'Allowed actions: {", ".join(it["allowed"])}'])
 142  
 143  
 144  def messages(s, it, order):
 145      return [{'role': 'system', 'content': b2.SYSTEM['V3']},
 146              {'role': 'user', 'content': state(s, it) + '\n\n' + b2.tail('V3', b2.offered(it, order))}]
 147  
 148  
 149  def load_set(path, want):
 150      """The set's (data, SHA-256): refused inside the repo (checked before reading), on another hash, or when the
 151      content differs from build_set()."""
 152      if b2.inside_repo(path):
 153          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} is inside the repository (test-set custody)')
 154      data = Path(path).read_bytes()
 155      if sha(data) != want:
 156          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} SHA-256 {sha(data)[:12]} != pinned {str(want)[:12]}')
 157      s = json.loads(data)
 158      if s != build_set():
 159          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} differs from build_set()')
 160      return s, sha(data)
 161  
 162  
 163  def preflight(post, s, info):
 164      """prompts[cond][('V3', order, id)] = (text, ids, {name: id}); every prompt rendered and tokenized, no
 165      /completion; the scored position is checked with an empty reason (b2.score rechecks it with the real one)."""
 166      prompts, longest = {}, 0
 167      for cond in CONDITIONS:
 168          for order in ORDERS:
 169              for it in subset(s, cond)['items']:
 170                  names = b2.offered(it, order)
 171                  text, ids, bad = b0.render(post, messages(s, it, order))
 172                  t2 = text + b2.ACTION
 173                  nid, why = b2.first_ids(post, t2, b0.tokenize(post, t2), names, ' ')
 174                  info['problems'] += [f'{cond} {order} item {it["id"]}: {w}' for w in why + ([bad] if bad else [])]
 175                  prompts.setdefault(cond, {})['V3', order, it['id']] = (text, ids, nid)
 176                  longest = max(longest, len(ids))
 177                  info.setdefault('name_ids', {}).update(nid)
 178                  if (cond, it['id']) == SHOW and order == 'FWD':
 179                      info['prompt_show'] = post('/detokenize', {'tokens': ids})['content']
 180      info['longest_prompt'] = longest
 181      if longest + CTX_MARGIN > CTX:
 182          info['problems'].append(f'longest prompt {longest} + {CTX_MARGIN} > {CTX}')
 183      return prompts
 184  
 185  
 186  def run_all(post, s, pid, emit):
 187      rows, info = [], {'problems': []}
 188      try:
 189          prompts = preflight(post, s, info)
 190          info['n_ctx'] = post('/props', None).get('default_generation_settings', {}).get('n_ctx')
 191          if info['n_ctx'] != CTX:
 192              info['problems'].append(f'server n_ctx {info["n_ctx"]!r} != {CTX}')
 193          if info['problems']:
 194              info['stopped'] = 'STOP before any inference: ' + '; '.join(info['problems'])
 195              return rows, info
 196          for k, cond in enumerate(CONDITIONS):
 197              if not b2.run_blocks(post, subset(s, cond), [('V3', o) for o in ORDERS], prompts[cond], pid, emit, rows,
 198                                   info, k == 0):
 199                  break
 200      except Exception as e:
 201          info['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 202          info['stopped'] = f'exception after {len(rows)} requests: {info["exception"]}'
 203      return rows, info
 204  
 205  
 206  def metrics(rows, s):
 207      """m[cond][order] = {n, strict, ok (acceptable count), change (ok - NONE ok, same order), cites (ids)};
 208      m[cond]['flips'] = ids whose FWD and REV tops differ. A missing row counts as wrong."""
 209      r = {(x['set'], x['order'], x['id']): x for x in rows if 'top' in x}
 210      m = {}
 211      for cond in CONDITIONS:
 212          items = subset(s, cond)['items']
 213          m[cond] = {'flips': [it['id'] for it in items if r.get((cond, 'FWD', it['id']), {}).get('top')
 214                               != r.get((cond, 'REV', it['id']), {}).get('top')]}
 215          for o in ORDERS:
 216              got = {it['id']: r.get((cond, o, it['id']), {}) for it in items}
 217              m[cond][o] = {'n': len(items),
 218                            'strict': sum(got[it['id']].get('top') == it['strict'] for it in items),
 219                            'ok': sum(got[it['id']].get('top') in [it['strict'], *it['also_ok']] for it in items),
 220                            'cites': [i for i, x in got.items() if CITES_MEMORY.search(x.get('reason', ''))]}
 221      for cond in CONDITIONS:
 222          for o in ORDERS:
 223              m[cond][o]['change'] = m[cond][o]['ok'] - m['NONE'][o]['ok']
 224      return m
 225  
 226  
 227  def verdict(m):
 228      """{criterion: bool}, every criterion in both orders; overall PASS when all hold. Integer arithmetic."""
 229      c = {}
 230      for o in ORDERS:
 231          for cond, bar in PASS.items():
 232              x = m[cond][o]
 233              c[f'{cond} {o} acceptable >= {bar:.2f}'] = x['ok'] * 100 >= round(bar * 100) * x['n']
 234          for cond in HARM:
 235              x, none = m[cond][o], m['NONE'][o]
 236              c[f'{cond} {o} acceptable >= NONE - {HARM_MAX:.2f}'] = \
 237                  x['ok'] * 100 >= none['ok'] * 100 - round(HARM_MAX * 100) * x['n']
 238      return c
 239  
 240  
 241  def validity(rows, info, run):
 242      why = []
 243      n = len(CONDITIONS) * len(ORDERS) * len(BASES)
 244      if len(rows) != n or any('error' in r for r in rows):
 245          why.append(f'{len(rows)}/{n} rows, {sum("error" in r for r in rows)} errors')
 246      if len({(r['pid'], r['pid_start']) for r in rows}) > 1:
 247          why.append('more than one server PID')
 248      if run.get('exit_code') != 0 or run.get('killed'):
 249          why.append(f'server exit {run.get("exit_code")}, SIGKILL {run.get("killed")}')
 250      for k in ('exception', 'stopped'):
 251          if info.get(k) or run.get(k):
 252              why.append(f'{k}: {info.get(k) or run.get(k)}')
 253      if run.get('set_sha256_end') != run.get('set_sha256'):
 254          why.append(f'set SHA-256 at the end {run.get("set_sha256_end")} != start {run.get("set_sha256")}')
 255      return ('VALID' if not why else 'INVALID'), why
 256  
 257  
 258  def report(run, rows, info):
 259      label, why = run['validity']
 260      s = {'missions': MISSIONS, 'items': run['set_items']}
 261      L = [f'MIND-B3 {run["utc"]}  code {run["code_sha256"][:12]}  b2 {run["b2_sha256"][:12]}  base {run["git_head"]}',
 262           f'SET {run["set_sha256"]} ({len(run["set_items"])} rows, {run["set_path"]})',
 263           f'server: {" ".join(run["command"])}',
 264           f'n_ctx {info.get("n_ctx")}; longest prompt {info.get("longest_prompt")} tokens; name first-token ids '
 265           f'{info.get("name_ids")}',
 266           f'ACCURACY {label}' + (f': {"; ".join(why)}' if why else ' (temperature 0; 240/240 rows; one server PID; '
 267                                  'exit 0; set hash unchanged)'),
 268           '* timings NOT MEASUREMENTS (agent resident allowed)']
 269      L += [f'block {x["block"]}: {x["duration_s"]} s*' + ('  OVER ~4 min (continued)' if x['over_warn'] else '')
 270            for x in info.get('blocks', [])]
 271      m = metrics(rows, s)
 272      L += ['', 'condition    order  strict  acceptable  change vs NONE (items)  flips']
 273      for cond in CONDITIONS:
 274          for o in ORDERS:
 275              x = m[cond][o]
 276              L.append(f'{cond:<12} {o}    {x["strict"]:>2}/{x["n"]}   {x["ok"]:>2}/{x["n"]} {x["ok"] / x["n"]:.3f}  '
 277                       f'{x["change"]:+d}' + (f'                      {len(m[cond]["flips"])} {m[cond]["flips"]}'
 278                                              if o == 'FWD' else ''))
 279      L += ['', 'reasons citing a memory note or Rule 5 (regex '
 280            f'{CITES_MEMORY.pattern!r}) under NONE and IRRELEVANT (invented or misapplied memory):']
 281      by = {(r['set'], r['order'], r['id']): r for r in rows if 'top' in r}
 282      for cond in ('NONE', 'IRRELEVANT'):
 283          for o in ORDERS:
 284              ids = m[cond][o]['cites']
 285              L.append(f'  {cond} {o}: {len(ids)} {ids}')
 286              L += [f'    {i}: {by[cond, o, i]["reason"]!r}' for i in ids]
 287      c = verdict(m)
 288      overall = ('PASS' if all(c.values()) else 'FAIL') if label == 'VALID' else 'NO RESULT (run INVALID)'
 289      L += ['', 'PASS rule (every criterion in both orders):'] + [f'  {k}: {"PASS" if v else "FAIL"}'
 290                                                                 for k, v in c.items()]
 291      L += [f'  overall: {overall}', f'  NONE acceptable (no bar): FWD {m["NONE"]["FWD"]["ok"]}/20, REV '
 292                                     f'{m["NONE"]["REV"]["ok"]}/20']
 293      L += ['', f'sample reasons (FWD, ids {SAMPLE_IDS}; id: top (label) | reason):']
 294      for cond in CONDITIONS:
 295          L.append(f'  {cond}')
 296          for it in subset(s, cond)['items']:
 297              if it['id'] in SAMPLE_IDS and (r := by.get((cond, 'FWD', it['id']))):
 298                  L.append(f'    {it["id"]}: {r["top"]} ({it["strict"]}) | {r["reason"]!r}')
 299      L += ['', 'per item: id  label (also ok)  FWD top p | REV top p']
 300      for cond in CONDITIONS:
 301          L.append(f'  {cond}')
 302          for it in subset(s, cond)['items']:
 303              cells = [f'{r["top"]} {r["probs"][r["top"]]:.2f}' if (r := by.get((cond, o, it['id']))) else '-'
 304                       for o in ORDERS]
 305              lab = it['strict'] + (f' ({",".join(it["also_ok"])})' if it['also_ok'] else '')
 306              L.append(f'    {it["id"]:<3} {lab:<18} {cells[0]:<15} {cells[1]}')
 307      L += ['', 'all reasons (condition order id: top | stop | reason):']
 308      L += [f'  {r["set"]} {r["order"]} {r["id"]:<3} {r.get("top", "-"):<8} {r.get("reason_stop")} | {r["reason"]!r}'
 309            for r in rows if 'reason' in r]
 310      if 'prompt_show' in info:
 311          L += ['', f'--- rendered prompt, {SHOW[0]} FWD, base {SHOW[1]} (/detokenize) ---', info['prompt_show']]
 312      if info.get('stopped'):
 313          L.append(f'STOPPED: {info["stopped"]}')
 314      if run.get('exception'):
 315          L.append(f'EXCEPTION (runner): {run["exception"]}')
 316      L.append(f'server exit code {run.get("exit_code")}; SIGKILL needed {run.get("killed")}')
 317      return '\n'.join(L) + '\n'
 318  
 319  
 320  def finish(out, run, rows, info):
 321      """Validity, run.json, report.txt and artifacts.sha256, always (also after an exception)."""
 322      run['validity'] = validity(rows, info, run)
 323      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps({**run, 'info': info}, indent=1) + '\n')
 324      text = report(run, rows, info)
 325      (out / 'report.txt').write_text(text)
 326      (out / 'artifacts.sha256').write_text(''.join(f'{sha(p.read_bytes())}  {p.name}\n' for p in sorted(out.iterdir())
 327                                                    if p.name != 'artifacts.sha256'))
 328      return text
 329  
 330  
 331  def build():
 332      out = SET_ROOT / time.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ', time.gmtime())
 333      if b2.inside_repo(out):
 334          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {out} is inside the repository')
 335      out.mkdir(parents=True)  # a new folder; an existing one is an error
 336      data = (json.dumps(build_set(), indent=1) + '\n').encode()
 337      (out / 'b3_set.json').write_bytes(data)
 338      print(f'{out / "b3_set.json"}\nSHA-256 {sha(data)}\nrows {len(build_set()["items"])}')
 339  
 340  
 341  def show():
 342      s = build_set()
 343      it = next(x for x in subset(s, SHOW[0])['items'] if x['id'] == SHOW[1])
 344      print(b0.chat.build_prompt('gemma', messages(s, it, 'FWD')))
 345  
 346  
 347  def main():
 348      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
 349      ap.add_argument('cmd', nargs='?', choices=['build', 'show', 'run'], default='run')
 350      ap.add_argument('--set', default=str(SET))
 351      a = ap.parse_args()
 352      if a.cmd != 'run':
 353          return build() if a.cmd == 'build' else show()
 354      s, set_sha = load_set(a.set, SET_SHA256)
 355      if bad := mem1.pinned_problems()[0]:
 356          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {bad}')
 357      if not b0.repro.port_free():
 358          raise SystemExit('refused: port 8080 in use (never stops a server it did not start)')
 359      utc = time.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ', time.gmtime())
 360      out = OUT_ROOT / utc
 361      out.mkdir(parents=True)
 362      run = {'utc': utc, 'set_path': a.set, 'set_sha256': set_sha, 'set_items': s['items'],
 363             'code_sha256': sha(Path(__file__).read_bytes()), 'b2_sha256': sha(Path(b2.__file__).read_bytes()),
 364             'b1_sha256': sha(Path(b1.__file__).read_bytes()), 'b0_sha256': sha(Path(b0.__file__).read_bytes()),
 365             'git_head': subprocess.run(['git', '-C', str(HERE), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], capture_output=True,
 366                                        text=True).stdout.strip(),
 367             'command': mem1.command(CTX), 'decode': {**b0.DECODE, **b0.PICK, 'cache_prompt': True},
 368             'reason': b2.REASON, 'system': b2.SYSTEM['V3'], 'timings': 'NOT MEASUREMENTS'}
 369      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')
 370      with open(out / 'requests.jsonl', 'x') as rq, open(out / 'server.log', 'x') as log:
 371          def emit(row):
 372              rq.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
 373              rq.flush()
 374          killed, rows, info, proc, exc = False, [], {}, None, None
 375          try:
 376              proc = subprocess.Popen(mem1.command(CTX), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
 377                                      start_new_session=True)
 378              t0 = time.monotonic()
 379              while not cr.healthy():
 380                  if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() - t0 > b1.LOAD_TIMEOUT_S:
 381                      raise RuntimeError(f'llama-server not healthy (exit {proc.poll()})')
 382                  time.sleep(0.1)
 383              run['server_pid'] = proc.pid
 384              rows, info = run_all(b1.Server(), s, proc.pid, emit)
 385          except BaseException as e:  # recorded; the report and manifest are still written, then a stop re-raises
 386              exc = e
 387              run['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 388          finally:
 389              if proc is not None and proc.poll() is None:
 390                  os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
 391                  try:
 392                      proc.wait(timeout=b1.TERM_WAIT_S)
 393                  except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
 394                      os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
 395                      proc.wait()
 396                      killed = True
 397              run.update(exit_code=proc.returncode if proc else None, killed=killed,
 398                         set_sha256_end=sha(Path(a.set).read_bytes()))
 399      print(finish(out, run, rows, info) + f'\n{out}')
 400      if exc is not None and not isinstance(exc, Exception):
 401          raise exc
 402  
 403  
 404  if __name__ == '__main__':
 405      main()
END

   1  """Offline tests for b3.py with B2's fake server: condition generation (MISLEADING pairing, date sorting, labels),
   2  memory rendering, a full fake run, metrics, the PASS rule, the invented-memory count, custody and the context STOP.
   3  No server, no phone."""
   4  import json
   5  import sys
   6  import tempfile
   7  import unittest
   8  from pathlib import Path
   9  
  10  sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
  11  sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
  12  import b3  # noqa: E402
  13  sys.path.insert(0, str(b3.HERE.parent / 'b2_rules'))
  14  import test_b2  # noqa: E402  B2's fake server (word-level tokens with leading space, V3 two steps)
  15  
  16  S = b3.build_set()
  17  
  18  
  19  def item(cond, i):
  20      return next(it for it in S['items'] if it['condition'] == cond and it['id'] == i)
  21  
  22  
  23  class Fake(test_b2.Fake):
  24      """Answers the reason with `reason(item)` and the action with `pick(item, offered)`; the item is the one whose
  25      rendered state is in the user turn."""
  26  
  27      def __init__(self, pick, reason=lambda it: 'Rule 13 fits'):
  28          super().__init__(lambda user, offered: pick(self.item(user), offered))
  29          self.reason = reason
  30  
  31      def item(self, user):
  32          return next(it for it in S['items'] if b3.state(S, it) + '\n' in user)
  33  
  34      def __call__(self, path, body=None):
  35          if path == '/completion' and body['n_predict'] == 48:
  36              self.bodies.append(body)
  37              user = self.text(body['prompt']).split('<|turn>user')[1]
  38              return {'content': self.reason(self.item(user)), 'tokens_predicted': 3, 'stop_type': 'word',
  39                      'timings': {'prompt_n': 5, 'cache_n': 0}}
  40          return super().__call__(path, body)
  41  
  42  
  43  def label(it, offered):
  44      return it['strict']
  45  
  46  
  47  def run(fake):
  48      b3.b2.PAUSE_S = 0
  49      events = []
  50      rows, info = b3.run_all(b3.b1.Server(fake), json.loads(json.dumps(S)), 7, events.append)
  51      return rows, info, events
  52  
  53  
  54  def rows_with(ok):  # ok[cond] = acceptable count, same in both orders; the first ok items right, the rest 'look'
  55      rows = []
  56      for cond in b3.CONDITIONS:
  57          for o in b3.ORDERS:
  58              for it in b3.subset(S, cond)['items']:
  59                  top = it['strict'] if it['id'] <= ok[cond] else 'look' if it['strict'] != 'look' else 'wait'
  60                  rows.append({'set': cond, 'order': o, 'id': it['id'], 'top': top, 'reason': 'Rule 13 fits'})
  61      return rows
  62

  64  class T(unittest.TestCase):
  65      def test_set(self):
  66          self.assertEqual(len(S['items']), 120)
  67          self.assertEqual([it['condition'] for it in S['items'][::20]], b3.CONDITIONS)
  68          self.assertTrue(all(it['allowed'] == b3.NAMES for it in S['items']))
  69          n = {i: b[3] for i, b in b3.BASES.items()}
  70          for i in b3.BASES:
  71              self.assertEqual(item('NONE', i)['memory'], [])
  72              self.assertEqual(item('RELEVANT', i)['memory'], [['Oct 20', n[i]]])
  73              self.assertEqual([d for d, _ in item('MIXED', i)['memory']], ['Oct 12', 'Oct 15', 'Oct 18', 'Oct 20',
  74                                                                            'Oct 25'])
  75              self.assertEqual(item('MIXED', i)['memory'][3], ['Oct 20', n[i]])
  76              self.assertEqual(item('IRRELEVANT', i)['memory'], [list(p) for p in b3.POOL])
  77              self.assertEqual(item('STALE', i)['memory'], [['Oct 20', n[i]], ['Nov 1', 'The Oct 20 note is no longer '
  78                                                                                        'true.']])
  79              j = i + 10 if i <= 10 else i - 10
  80              self.assertEqual(item('MISLEADING', i)['memory'], [['Oct 20', n[j]]])
  81              self.assertNotEqual(j, i)
  82              b = b3.BASES[i]
  83              for cond in b3.CONDITIONS:
  84                  it = item(cond, i)
  85                  self.assertEqual(it['strict'], b[5] if cond in ('RELEVANT', 'MIXED') else b[4])
  86                  self.assertEqual(it['also_ok'], ['greet'] if i == 20 and cond in ('RELEVANT', 'MIXED') else [])
  87          self.assertEqual(item('MISLEADING', 1)['memory'][0][1], n[11])
  88          self.assertEqual(item('MISLEADING', 20)['memory'][0][1], n[10])
  89          self.assertEqual(b3.by_date([('Nov 1', 'a'), ('Oct 25', 'b'), ('Oct 3', 'c')]),
  90                           [('Oct 3', 'c'), ('Oct 25', 'b'), ('Nov 1', 'a')])  # by date, not by text
  91  
  92      def test_rendering(self):
  93          self.assertEqual(b3.state(S, item('NONE', 8)), "Mission: Map the flat quietly and do not disturb anyone.\n"
  94                           "Current activity: exploring\nWhat just happened: A person is at Lisa's desk, at medium "
  95                           "distance, silent.\nMemory: none\nAllowed actions: explore, approach, greet, look, wait")
  96          st = b3.state(S, item('STALE', 14))
  97          self.assertIn('Current activity: waiting\n', st)
  98          self.assertIn("\nMemory:\n- Oct 20: When Max says he'll be right back, he is gone for hours.\n"
  99                        '- Nov 1: The Oct 20 note is no longer true.\nAllowed actions:', st)
 100          m = b3.messages(S, item('MIXED', 8), 'REV')
 101          self.assertEqual(m[0]['content'], b3.b2.SYSTEM['V3'])
 102          self.assertEqual(m[1]['content'], b3.state(S, item('MIXED', 8)) + '\n\n' + b3.b2.tail(
 103              'V3', ['wait', 'look', 'greet', 'approach', 'explore']))

 105      def test_full_run_and_report(self):
 106          fake = Fake(label, lambda it: 'Rule 5: the memory note decides' if it['condition'] == 'IRRELEVANT'
 107                      and it['id'] == 3 else 'Rule 12 fits')
 108          rows, info, events = run(fake)
 109          self.assertNotIn('stopped', info)
 110          self.assertEqual(len(rows), 240)
 111          self.assertEqual([(r['set'], r['order']) for r in rows[::20]], [(c, o) for c in b3.CONDITIONS
 112                                                                          for o in b3.ORDERS])
 113          self.assertEqual(len(fake.bodies), 480)  # two steps per row
 114          self.assertIn('- Oct 25: The plants', info['prompt_show'])
 115          m = b3.metrics(rows, S)
 116          self.assertEqual(m['IRRELEVANT']['FWD']['cites'], [3])
 117          self.assertEqual(m['NONE']['FWD']['cites'], [])
 118          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 119              run_rec = {'utc': 'T', 'code_sha256': 'c' * 64, 'b2_sha256': 'b' * 64, 'git_head': 'h', 'set_path': '/s',
 120                         'set_sha256': 's' * 64, 'set_sha256_end': 's' * 64, 'set_items': S['items'], 'command': ['x'],
 121                         'exit_code': 0, 'killed': False}
 122              text = b3.finish(Path(d), dict(run_rec), rows, info)
 123              self.assertIn('ACCURACY VALID', text)
 124              self.assertIn('overall: PASS', text)
 125              self.assertIn("IRRELEVANT FWD: 1 [3]\n    3: 'Rule 5: the memory note decides'", text)
 126              self.assertIn('rendered prompt, MIXED FWD, base 8', text)
 127              text = b3.finish(Path(d), {**run_rec, 'set_sha256_end': 'z'}, rows[:-1], info)
 128              self.assertIn('ACCURACY INVALID: 239/240 rows', text)
 129              self.assertIn('NO RESULT', text)
 130  
 131      def test_metrics_and_pass(self):
 132          ok = {'NONE': 18, 'RELEVANT': 16, 'MIXED': 15, 'IRRELEVANT': 16, 'STALE': 17, 'MISLEADING': 20}
 133          m = b3.metrics(rows_with(ok), S)
 134          self.assertEqual((m['NONE']['FWD']['ok'], m['IRRELEVANT']['REV']['change']), (18, -2))
 135          self.assertEqual(m['MIXED']['flips'], [])
 136          c = b3.verdict(m)
 137          self.assertEqual(len(c), 10)
 138          self.assertTrue(all(c.values()))  # all on their bars
 139          for cond, v in (('RELEVANT', 15), ('MIXED', 14), ('IRRELEVANT', 15), ('STALE', 15), ('MISLEADING', 15)):
 140              c = b3.verdict(b3.metrics(rows_with({**ok, cond: v}), S))
 141              self.assertEqual([k for k, x in c.items() if not x], [k for k in c if k.startswith(cond + ' ')])
 142          rows = rows_with(ok)
 143          rows[0]['top'] = 'greet'  # NONE FWD item 1: wrong in FWD only
 144          m = b3.metrics(rows, S)
 145          self.assertEqual((m['NONE']['flips'], m['NONE']['FWD']['ok'], m['NONE']['REV']['ok']), ([1], 17, 18))
 146          self.assertEqual(m['IRRELEVANT']['FWD']['change'], -1)
 147          self.assertEqual(b3.metrics([r for r in rows if r['id'] != 2], S)['NONE']['FWD']['ok'], 16)  # missing = wrong
 148          ok20 = {**ok, 'RELEVANT': 20}
 149          rows = rows_with(ok20)
 150          next(r for r in rows if (r['set'], r['id']) == ('RELEVANT', 20))['top'] = 'greet'
 151          self.assertEqual(b3.metrics(rows, S)['RELEVANT']['FWD']['strict'], 19)
 152          self.assertEqual(b3.metrics(rows, S)['RELEVANT']['FWD']['ok'], 20)  # also ok
 153  
 154      def test_cites_memory(self):
 155          hit = ['Rule 5 fits.', 'rule 5 applies', 'The memory says to greet.', 'A note says so.', 'I remember that.']
 156          miss = ['Rule 12 fits.', 'Rule 15 fits.', 'Nothing new happened; rule 13.', 'I notice a person.']
 157          self.assertTrue(all(b3.CITES_MEMORY.search(r) for r in hit))
 158          self.assertFalse(any(b3.CITES_MEMORY.search(r) for r in miss))
 159  
 160      def test_custody(self):
 161          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 162              p = Path(d) / 'b3_set.json'
 163              data = (json.dumps(S, indent=1) + '\n').encode()
 164              p.write_bytes(data)
 165              self.assertEqual(b3.load_set(p, b3.sha(data)), (S, b3.sha(data)))
 166              with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, 'pinned'):
 167                  b3.load_set(p, '0' * 64)
 168              bad = json.loads(data)
 169              bad['items'][0]['strict'] = 'wait'
 170              p.write_text(json.dumps(bad))
 171              with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, 'differs'):
 172                  b3.load_set(p, b3.sha(p.read_bytes()))
 173              link = Path(d) / 'link.json'
 174              link.symlink_to(b3.HERE / 'b3.py')
 175              with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, 'inside the repository'):
 176                  b3.load_set(link, None)
 177  
 178      def test_context_stop(self):
 179          fake = Fake(label)
 180          b3.CTX = 100
 181          try:
 182              rows, info, events = run(fake)
 183          finally:
 184              b3.CTX = 2048
 185          self.assertIn('STOP before any inference', info['stopped'])
 186          self.assertIn('+ 64 > 100', info['stopped'])
 187          self.assertEqual((rows, fake.bodies, events), ([], [], []))
 188  
 189  
 190  if __name__ == '__main__':
 191      unittest.main()
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b3_memory/README.md (34 lines)
   1  # MIND-B3 — does memory help the picker, and can bad memory hurt it?
   2  
   3  Research only (after MIND-B2). Using B2's selected picker unchanged (V3: rules + 13 worked examples + one reason
   4  sentence, two-step scoring), does a relevant memory note make Gemma 4 E2B choose the right action, and do irrelevant,
   5  outdated or wrong notes make it worse than no memory? Accuracy is deterministic (temperature 0; agent resident
   6  allowed); timings are recorded but are NOT MEASUREMENTS.
   7  
   8  - Imports `b2.py` unchanged (`SYSTEM['V3']`, `tail`, `score`, `first_ids`, `run_blocks`, `inside_repo`), and through
   9    it B1 and B0. Only the memory line differs from B2: `Memory: none`, or `Memory:` followed by `- <date>: <note>`
  10    lines, oldest first. All five actions allowed in every item, catalogue order; FWD/REV as B2.
  11  - Set: 20 owner-written bases × 6 conditions (NONE, RELEVANT, MIXED, IRRELEVANT, STALE, MISLEADING) = 120 rows,
  12    generated by `build_set()` and written OUTSIDE the repo by `b3.py build` before any Gemma call:
  13    `/termux-home/mind_b3_set/20261102T193300Z/b3_set.json`, SHA-256
  14    `156542981e89e7afd0a2b833faf989449b9c11cc750b73fddf16f3a0cf4217ab`, 120 rows. `b3.py` pins path and hash and
  15    refuses a set inside the repo (B2's fixed `inside_repo`), another hash, or content different from `build_set()`.
  16  - Conditions and labels: see the docstring of `b3.py`. MISLEADING gives base i the note of base j = i+10 (i ≤ 10)
  17    or i−10; "also ok" (greet) only for base 20 under RELEVANT and MIXED.
  18  - Run: one launch (setup_q4 flags, ctx 2048); every prompt checked before inference (template, distinct first
  19    tokens, prompt + 64 ≤ 2048, else STOP); 12 blocks of 20 items, NONE, RELEVANT, MIXED, IRRELEVANT, STALE,
  20    MISLEADING, FWD then REV each, 60 s pause between blocks.
  21  - Metrics per condition × order: strict and acceptable, change vs NONE (acceptable items, same order), order flips,
  22    reasons citing a memory note or Rule 5 under NONE and IRRELEVANT (regex
  23    `\bmemor(y|ies)\b|\bnotes?\b|\bremember|\brule\s*5\b`, case-insensitive; every match is listed in report.txt for
  24    a human check, since a reason such as "no memory applies" also matches), 5 sample reasons per condition (FWD,
  25    bases 1, 5, 9, 13, 17).
  26  - PASS (fixed before the first Gemma call; every criterion in both orders): RELEVANT acceptable ≥ 0.80; MIXED
  27    acceptable ≥ 0.75; IRRELEVANT, STALE and MISLEADING each acceptable ≥ NONE acceptable − 0.10 (at most 2 of 20
  28    worse). NONE has no bar. Integer comparisons.
  29  - Commands (Termux python, works from proot Debian): `python b3.py build` (new set folder), `python b3.py show`
  30    (rendered MIXED prompt, base 8, offline), `python b3.py` (the run; writes `~/robot_runs/mind_b3/<UTC>/`: run.json,
  31    requests.jsonl, server.log, report.txt, artifacts.sha256).
  32  - Offline tests: `python -B test_b3.py` (B2's fake server; no phone).
  33  - Known limitation carried over from B0: an interrupt or a server-stop error can skip report.txt and
  34    artifacts.sha256 (completed rows stay in requests.jsonl).
END

BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/b2.py (488 lines)
   1  #!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/python
   2  """MIND-B2: picker quality with the policy written as explicit rules (V1), rules + worked examples (V2), rules +
   3  examples + one reason sentence first (V3). Research only: no motors, camera, YOLO, M2, main.py or run_mission.py; no
   4  MTP, no projector. Accuracy is deterministic (temperature 0); timings are recorded but are NOT MEASUREMENTS.
   5  
   6  Actions: explore, approach (B1's visit renamed), greet, look, wait. Only allowed actions are offered and scored,
   7  renormalised over them; ties: first offered.
   8  DEV = b1_quality/set/b1_set.json (SHA-256 pinned, read 'visit' as 'approach'). TEST = 40 items kept OUTSIDE the repo
   9  (WORKFLOW test-set custody) at ~/mind_b2_test/b2_test.json, SHA-256 pinned below (recorded before any Gemma call);
  10  load_test() refuses to open it until selection.json exists and its logged SHA-256 matches; a TEST path inside the repo
  11  (also through a symlink) is refused before it is read.
  12  Rendering: Gemma 4 template (b0.render, checked against /apply-template, thinking off); system = SYS_RULES (V1) or
  13  SYS_RULES + 'Examples:' (V2, V3); user = B1 arm B state lines (gerund activity, Allowed actions in catalogue order), a
  14  blank line, the tail with the allowed names (FWD catalogue order, REV reversed); cache_prompt true.
  15    V1, V2: first-token scoring over the names right after the model turn.
  16    V3: step 1 generates the reason (n_predict 48, stop at the first newline); step 2 appends '\\nAction:' to the model
  17        turn and scores the next token over ' <name>' (the names after a space, as 'Action: wait' tokenizes).
  18  Names must have distinct first tokens in the scored position: checked with /tokenize for every DEV prompt before any
  19  inference (V3 with an empty reason), again for each V3 item with its actual reason, and for the TEST prompts before
  20  TEST inference; a problem STOPs (INVALID).
  21  One server launch (mem1.command(2048) = setup_q4 flags, pins checked); DEV blocks V1-FWD .. V3-REV; select() (fixed
  22  rule) -> selection.json, SHA-256 logged; TEST: the selected variant FWD then REV. PAUSE_S between blocks. Metrics per
  23  block are B1's (b1.metrics); PASS on TEST is B1's rule (acceptable >= 0.80 and strict >= 0.65 in both orders, flips
  24  <= 4). VALID only with all 320 rows without error, one server PID, exit 0 without SIGKILL, no exception or stop, and
  25  both set hashes unchanged at the end.
  26  
  27  Usage: b2.py [--test PATH]   (Termux python; from proot Debian or native Termux)
  28  Writes ~/robot_runs/mind_b2/<UTC>/: run.json, requests.jsonl, selection.json, server.log, report.txt, artifacts.sha256.
  29  """

 114  SYSTEM = {'V1': SYS_RULES, 'V2': SYS_RULES + '\n\nExamples:\n' + '\n'.join(EXAMPLES)}

 117  
 118  def inside_repo(path):
 119      p = Path(path).resolve()  # symlinks resolved (B1 review P1)
 120      return any(q.exists() and os.path.samefile(q, REPO) for q in [p, *p.parents])  # bind mounts: same inode
 121  
 122  
 123  def load_set(path, want, custody=True):
 124      """(set, bytes SHA-256): refused when custody and the path is inside the repo (checked before reading) or the
 125      hash differs. 'visit' in labels, current activity and allowed lists reads 'approach'."""
 126      if custody and inside_repo(path):
 127          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} is inside the repository (test-set custody)')
 128      data = Path(path).read_bytes()
 129      if want is not None and sha(data) != want:
 130          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {path} SHA-256 {sha(data)[:12]} != pinned {want[:12]}')
 131      s = json.loads(data)
 132      ren = {'visit': 'approach'}
 133      for it in s['items']:
 134          it['current'], it['strict'] = ren.get(it['current'], it['current']), ren.get(it['strict'], it['strict'])
 135          it['allowed'], it['also_ok'] = [ren.get(n, n) for n in it['allowed']], [ren.get(n, n) for n in it['also_ok']]
 136      return s, sha(data)
 137  
 138  
 139  def load_test(out, logged_sha, path):
 140      """The TEST set, only after selection.json exists with the logged SHA-256."""
 141      sel = out / 'selection.json'
 142      if not logged_sha or not sel.exists() or sha(sel.read_bytes()) != logged_sha:
 143          raise SystemExit('refused: TEST is locked until selection.json is written and its SHA-256 logged')
 144      return load_set(path, TEST_SHA256)
 145  
 146  
 147  def state(s, it):
 148      return '\n'.join([f'Mission: {s["missions"][it["mission"]]}', f'Current activity: {ACTIVITY[it["current"]]}',
 149                        f'What just happened: {it["event"]}', f'Memory: {it["memory"] or "none"}',
 150                        f'Allowed actions: {", ".join(it["allowed"])}'])
 151  
 152  
 153  def offered(it, order):
 154      return it['allowed'] if order == 'FWD' else it['allowed'][::-1]
 155  
 156  
 157  def tail(variant, names):
 158      words = f'{", ".join(names[:-1])} or {names[-1]}.'
 159      if variant == 'V3':
 160          return ('Which action next? First write one short sentence naming the rule that fits. Then write a new line '
 161                  f"starting with 'Action:' and one word: {words}")
 162      return f'Which action next? Answer with one word: {words}'
 163  
 164  
 165  def messages(s, it, variant, order):
 166      return [{'role': 'system', 'content': SYSTEM[variant]},
 167              {'role': 'user', 'content': state(s, it) + '\n\n' + tail(variant, offered(it, order))}]
 168  
 169  
 170  def first_ids(post, text, ids, names, sep=''):
 171      """({name: first token id after text}, problems): text's ids must stay a prefix; first tokens distinct."""
 172      out, why = {}, []
 173      for n in names:
 174          full = b0.tokenize(post, text + sep + n)
 175          if full[:len(ids)] != ids or len(full) == len(ids):
 176              why.append(f'{n!r}: prompt ids change or no token follows')
 177          else:
 178              out[n] = full[len(ids)]
 179      if len(set(out.values())) != len(out):
 180          why.append(f'first tokens not distinct: {out}')
 181      return out, why
 182  
 183  
 184  def preflight(post, s, blocks, info, tag):
 185      """prompts[(variant, order, id)] = (text, ids, {name: id}); every prompt rendered and tokenized, no /completion.
 186      Problems go to info['problems']."""
 187      prompts, sizes = {}, {}
 188      for v, order in blocks:
 189          for it in s['items']:
 190              names = offered(it, order)
 191              text, ids, bad = b0.render(post, messages(s, it, v, order))
 192              if v == 'V3':  # scored position checked with an empty reason; rechecked per item with the real one
 193                  t2 = text + ACTION
 194                  nid, why = first_ids(post, t2, b0.tokenize(post, t2), names, ' ')
 195              else:
 196                  nid, why = first_ids(post, text, ids, names)
 197              info['problems'] += [f'{tag} {v}-{order} item {it["id"]}: {w}' for w in why + ([bad] if bad else [])]
 198              prompts[v, order, it['id']] = (text, ids, nid)
 199              sizes.setdefault(v, []).append(len(ids))
 200              info.setdefault('name_ids', {}).setdefault('V3' if v == 'V3' else 'V1/V2', {}).update(nid)
 201              if tag == 'DEV' and it['id'] == SHOW_ITEM and order == 'FWD':
 202                  info[f'prompt_{v}_dev_item{SHOW_ITEM}'] = post('/detokenize', {'tokens': ids})['content']
 203      for v in sizes:
 204          info.setdefault('system_tokens', {})[v] = len(b0.tokenize(post, SYSTEM[v], special=False))
 205          info.setdefault('prompt_tokens', {})[f'{tag} {v}'] = (min(sizes[v]), max(sizes[v]))
 206          if max(sizes[v]) + CTX_MARGIN > CTX:
 207              info['problems'].append(f'{tag} {v}: longest prompt {max(sizes[v])} + {CTX_MARGIN} > {CTX}')
 208      return prompts
 209  
 210  
 211  def score(post, it, v, order, prompt):
 212      """One row: probabilities over the offered names (raw and renormalised), top, margin, counters; V3 adds the
 213      reason and its step-1 counters."""
 214      names, (text, ids, nid) = offered(it, order), prompt
 215      t0, row = time.perf_counter(), {'variant': v, 'order': order, 'id': it['id'], 'offered': names}
 216      if v == 'V3':
 217          r1 = post('/completion', {'prompt': ids, **b0.DECODE, **REASON, 'cache_prompt': True})
 218          reason = r1['content']
 219          t2 = text + reason + ACTION
 220          ids = b0.tokenize(post, t2)
 221          nid, why = first_ids(post, t2, ids, names, ' ')
 222          if why:
 223              raise RuntimeError(f'scored position after the reason: {why}')
 224          tm1 = r1.get('timings', {})
 225          row.update(reason=reason, reason_tokens=r1.get('tokens_predicted'), reason_stop=r1.get('stop_type'),
 226                     reason_prompt_n=tm1.get('prompt_n'), reason_cache_n=tm1.get('cache_n'))
 227      body = {'prompt': ids, **b0.DECODE, **b0.PICK, 'cache_prompt': True}
 228      res = post('/completion', body)
 229      raw, norm = b0.probs(res, {n: [nid[n]] for n in names})
 230      if norm is None:
 231          raise RuntimeError(f'item {it["id"]}: an offered name is outside the top {b0.PICK["n_probs"]}: {raw}')
 232      top = max(norm, key=norm.get)  # ties: first offered
 233      second = sorted(norm.values())[-2] if len(norm) > 1 else 0.0
 234      tm = res.get('timings', {})
 235      row.update(prompt_tokens=len(ids), prompt_ids_sha256=sha(json.dumps(ids)), prompt_n=tm.get('prompt_n'),
 236                 cache_n=tm.get('cache_n'), prompt_ms=tm.get('prompt_ms'), wall_s=round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 3),
 237                 probs_raw=raw, probs=norm, top=top, margin=norm[top] - second)
 238      return row
 239  
 240  
 241  def block_metrics(rows, s, v):
 242      """B1's metrics for one variant: (FWD block, REV block, {'flips', 'verdict'})."""
 243      # ponytail: reuses b1.metrics by relabelling the variant as B1's arm A; own metrics if B1's layout ever changes
 244      m = b1.metrics([{**r, 'arm': 'A'} for r in rows if r.get('variant') == v and r.get('set') == s['tag']], s)
 245      return m['blocks']['A-FWD'], m['blocks']['A-REV'], m['arms']['A']
 246  
 247  
 248  def select(dev):
 249      """Fixed rule. dev[v] = (FWD, REV, arm) from block_metrics. Among variants with flips <= MAX_FLIPS the highest
 250      mean acceptable over both orders; tie -> V1, then V2, then V3. None qualifies: highest mean anyway, flag FLIPS."""
 251      mean = {v: (dev[v][0]['acceptable'] + dev[v][1]['acceptable']) / 2 for v in VARIANTS}
 252      ok = [v for v in VARIANTS if len(dev[v][2]['flips']) <= MAX_FLIPS]
 253      pool = ok or VARIANTS
 254      best = max(pool, key=lambda v: (mean[v], -VARIANTS.index(v)))
 255      return {'variant': best, 'flag': None if ok else 'FLIPS', 'rule': select.__doc__.split('. ', 1)[1],
 256              'dev': {v: {'FWD': {k: dev[v][0][k] for k in ('strict', 'acceptable')},
 257                          'REV': {k: dev[v][1][k] for k in ('strict', 'acceptable')},
 258                          'mean_acceptable': mean[v], 'flips': dev[v][2]['flips']} for v in VARIANTS}}
 259  
 260  
 261  def run_blocks(post, s, blocks, prompts, pid, emit, rows, info, first):
 262      """Score the blocks in order; False at the first request error (recorded, the run stops)."""
 263      for k, (v, order) in enumerate(blocks):
 264          if k or not first:
 265              time.sleep(PAUSE_S)
 266          t0 = time.monotonic()
 267          for it in s['items']:
 268              try:
 269                  row = score(post, it, v, order, prompts[v, order, it['id']])
 270              except Exception as e:  # fail closed: recorded, the run ends
 271                  row = {'variant': v, 'order': order, 'id': it['id'], 'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'}
 272              row['set'], row['pid'], row['pid_start'] = s['tag'], pid, b0.pid_start(pid)
 273              rows.append(row)
 274              emit(row)
 275              if 'error' in row:
 276                  info['stopped'] = f'{s["tag"]} {v}-{order} item {it["id"]} failed: {row["error"]}'
 277                  return False
 278          dur = round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)
 279          info.setdefault('blocks', []).append({'block': f'{s["tag"]} {v}-{order}', 'duration_s': dur,
 280                                                'over_warn': dur > BLOCK_WARN_S})
 281      return True
 282  
 283  
 284  def run_all(post, dev, test_path, out, pid, emit):
 285      """DEV preflight and blocks, selection.json, then TEST (only now opened) for the selected variant.
 286      Returns (rows, info); info['test'] = TEST set and hash when it was opened."""
 287      rows, info = [], {'problems': []}
 288      try:
 289          dev['tag'] = 'DEV'
 290          prompts = preflight(post, dev, DEV_BLOCKS, info, 'DEV')
 291          n_ctx = post('/props', None).get('default_generation_settings', {}).get('n_ctx')
 292          info['n_ctx'] = n_ctx
 293          if n_ctx != CTX:
 294              info['problems'].append(f'server n_ctx {n_ctx!r} != {CTX}')
 295          if info['problems']:
 296              info['stopped'] = 'STOP before any inference: ' + '; '.join(info['problems'])
 297              return rows, info
 298          if not run_blocks(post, dev, DEV_BLOCKS, prompts, pid, emit, rows, info, True):
 299              return rows, info
 300          sel = select({v: block_metrics(rows, dev, v) for v in VARIANTS})
 301          (out / 'selection.json').write_text(json.dumps(sel, indent=1) + '\n')
 302          info['selection'], info['selection_sha256'] = sel, sha((out / 'selection.json').read_bytes())
 303          emit({'event': 'selection', 'variant': sel['variant'], 'flag': sel['flag'],
 304                'selection_sha256': info['selection_sha256']})
 305          test, info['test_sha256'] = load_test(out, info['selection_sha256'], test_path)
 306          test['tag'], info['test'] = 'TEST', test
 307          blocks = [(sel['variant'], 'FWD'), (sel['variant'], 'REV')]
 308          prompts = preflight(post, test, blocks, info, 'TEST')
 309          if info['problems']:
 310              info['stopped'] = 'STOP before TEST inference: ' + '; '.join(info['problems'])
 311              return rows, info
 312          run_blocks(post, test, blocks, prompts, pid, emit, rows, info, False)
 313      except Exception as e:
 314          info['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 315          info['stopped'] = f'exception after {len(rows)} requests: {info["exception"]}'
 316      return rows, info
 317  
 318  
 319  def validity(rows, info, run):
 320      why = []
 321      n = len(DEV_BLOCKS) * run['dev_rows'] + 2 * run.get('test_rows', 40)
 322      if len(rows) != n or any('error' in r for r in rows):
 323          why.append(f'{len(rows)}/{n} rows, {sum("error" in r for r in rows)} errors')
 324      if len({(r['pid'], r['pid_start']) for r in rows}) > 1:
 325          why.append('more than one server PID')
 326      if run.get('exit_code') != 0 or run.get('killed'):
 327          why.append(f'server exit {run.get("exit_code")}, SIGKILL {run.get("killed")}')
 328      for k in ('exception', 'stopped'):
 329          if info.get(k) or run.get(k):
 330              why.append(f'{k}: {info.get(k) or run.get(k)}')
 331      for k in ('dev', 'test'):
 332          if run.get(f'{k}_sha256_end') != run.get(f'{k}_sha256'):
 333              why.append(f'{k} SHA-256 at the end {run.get(f"{k}_sha256_end")} != start {run.get(f"{k}_sha256")}')
 334      return ('VALID' if not why else 'INVALID'), why
 335  
 336  
 337  def table(L, title, rows, s, variants):
 338      L += ['', title, 'block      strict        acceptable    copy rate      near-ties (margin < 0.05)']
 339      res = {}
 340      for v in variants:
 341          F, R, arm = res[v] = block_metrics(rows, s, v)
 342          for o, x in (('FWD', F), ('REV', R)):
 343              L.append(f'{v}-{o:<7} {x["strict"]:.3f} {round(x["strict"] * x["n"]):>2}/{x["n"]}  '
 344                       f'{x["acceptable"]:.3f} {round(x["acceptable"] * x["n"]):>2}/{x["n"]}  '
 345                       f'{(x["copy_rate"] or 0):.3f} {x["copy"][0]:>2}/{x["copy"][1]}  {x["near_ties"]}')
 346      L.append('recall per strict label (hits/n):')
 347      L += [f'  {v}-{o:<4} ' + '  '.join(f'{a} {h}/{n}' for a, (h, n) in x['recall'].items())
 348            for v in variants for o, x in zip(('FWD', 'REV'), res[v][:2])]
 349      L.append('confusion (rows strict, columns chosen):')
 350      L += [f'  {v}-{o:<4} ' + '; '.join(f'{a} -> ' + ', '.join(f'{c} {n}' for c, n in sorted(r.items()))
 351                                         for a, r in sorted(x['confusion'].items()))
 352            for v in variants for o, x in zip(('FWD', 'REV'), res[v][:2])]
 353      L.append('copied current activity (ids): ' + '; '.join(f'{v}-{o} {x["copied_ids"]}' for v in variants
 354                                                             for o, x in zip(('FWD', 'REV'), res[v][:2])))
 355      L += [f'flips {v}: {len(res[v][2]["flips"])} {res[v][2]["flips"]}' for v in variants]
 356      by = {(r['variant'], r['order'], r['id']): r for r in rows if r.get('set') == s['tag']}
 357      L.append('id  strict (also ok)       ' + ''.join(f'{v}-{o:<12}' for v in variants for o in ('FWD', 'REV')))
 358      for it in s['items']:
 359          cells = []
 360          for v in variants:
 361              for o in ('FWD', 'REV'):
 362                  r = by.get((v, o, it['id']), {})
 363                  cells.append(f'{r["top"]} {r["probs"][r["top"]]:.2f}' if 'top' in r else r.get('error', '-')[:14])
 364          lab = it['strict'] + (f' ({",".join(it["also_ok"])})' if it['also_ok'] else '')
 365          L.append(f'{it["id"]:<3} {lab:<22} ' + ''.join(f'{c:<15}' for c in cells))
 366      reasons = [r for r in rows if r.get('set') == s['tag'] and 'reason' in r]
 367      if reasons:
 368          L.append('V3 reasons (id order: top | stop | reason):')
 369          L += [f'  {r["order"]} {r["id"]:<3} {r.get("top", "-"):<8} {r.get("reason_stop")} | {r["reason"]!r}'
 370                for r in reasons]
 371      return res
 372  
 373  
 374  def report(run, rows, info):
 375      s_label, why = run['validity']
 376      L = [f'MIND-B2 {run["utc"]}  code {run["code_sha256"][:12]}  base {run["git_head"]}',
 377           f'DEV {run["dev_sha256"][:12]} ({run["dev_rows"]} rows, {run["dev_path"]}); TEST '
 378           f'{(run.get("test_sha256") or "not opened")[:12]} ({run["test_path"]})',
 379           f'server: {" ".join(run["command"])}',
 380           f'n_ctx {info.get("n_ctx")}; system tokens {info.get("system_tokens")}; prompt tokens (min, max) '
 381           f'{info.get("prompt_tokens")}',
 382           f'name first-token ids {info.get("name_ids")}',
 383           f'ACCURACY {s_label}' + (f': {"; ".join(why)}' if why else ' (temperature 0; 320/320 rows; one server PID; '
 384                                    'exit 0; set hashes unchanged)'),
 385           '* timings NOT MEASUREMENTS (agent resident allowed)']
 386      L += [f'block {x["block"]}: {x["duration_s"]} s*' + ('  OVER ~4 min (continued)' if x['over_warn'] else '')
 387            for x in info.get('blocks', [])]
 388      dev = {'tag': 'DEV', 'items': run['dev_items']}
 389      if any(r.get('set') == 'DEV' for r in rows):
 390          table(L, '=== DEV ===', rows, dev, VARIANTS)
 391      if sel := info.get('selection'):
 392          L += ['', f'SELECTION ({sel["rule"]}): {sel["variant"]}' + (f'  FLAG {sel["flag"]}' if sel['flag'] else '')
 393                + f'; selection.json SHA-256 {info["selection_sha256"]}']
 394          L += [f'  {v}: mean acceptable {d["mean_acceptable"]:.3f}, flips {len(d["flips"])}'
 395                for v, d in sel['dev'].items()]
 396      if info.get('test'):
 397          t = info['test']
 398          res = table(L, f'=== TEST (official; {sel["variant"]}) ===', rows, t, [sel['variant']])
 399          F, R, arm = res[sel['variant']]
 400          verdict = arm['verdict'] if s_label == 'VALID' else 'NO RESULT (run INVALID)'
 401          L += ['', f'PASS rule: acceptable >= {b1.PASS["acceptable"]} and strict >= {b1.PASS["strict"]} in both orders, '
 402                    f'flips <= {b1.PASS["max_flips"]}/40',
 403                f'  TEST {sel["variant"]}: strict {F["strict"]:.3f}/{R["strict"]:.3f}, acceptable '
 404                f'{F["acceptable"]:.3f}/{R["acceptable"]:.3f}, flips {len(arm["flips"])} {arm["flips"]} -> {verdict}']
 405      if f'prompt_V3_dev_item{SHOW_ITEM}' in info:
 406          L += ['', f'--- rendered prompt, V3 FWD, DEV item {SHOW_ITEM} (/detokenize) ---',
 407                info[f'prompt_V3_dev_item{SHOW_ITEM}']]
 408      if info.get('stopped'):
 409          L.append(f'STOPPED: {info["stopped"]}')
 410      if run.get('exception'):
 411          L.append(f'EXCEPTION (runner): {run["exception"]}')
 412      L.append(f'server exit code {run.get("exit_code")}; SIGKILL needed {run.get("killed")}')
 413      return '\n'.join(L) + '\n'
 414  
 415  
 416  def finish(out, run, rows, info):
 417      """Validity, run.json, report.txt and artifacts.sha256, always (also after an exception)."""
 418      run['validity'] = validity(rows, info, run)
 419      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps({**run, 'info': {k: v for k, v in info.items() if k != 'test'}},
 420                                               indent=1) + '\n')
 421      text = report(run, rows, info)
 422      (out / 'report.txt').write_text(text)
 423      (out / 'artifacts.sha256').write_text(''.join(f'{sha(p.read_bytes())}  {p.name}\n' for p in sorted(out.iterdir())
 424                                                    if p.name != 'artifacts.sha256'))
 425      return text
 426  
 427  
 428  def main():
 429      ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
 430      ap.add_argument('--test', default=str(TEST))
 431      a = ap.parse_args()
 432      if inside_repo(a.test):  # custody: refused before anything runs; the file itself is opened only after selection
 433          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {a.test} is inside the repository (test-set custody)')
 434      dev, dev_sha = load_set(DEV, DEV_SHA256, custody=False)
 435      if bad := mem1.pinned_problems()[0]:
 436          raise SystemExit(f'refused: {bad}')
 437      if not b0.repro.port_free():
 438          raise SystemExit('refused: port 8080 in use (never stops a server it did not start)')
 439      utc = time.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ', time.gmtime())
 440      out = OUT_ROOT / utc
 441      out.mkdir(parents=True)
 442      run = {'utc': utc, 'dev_path': str(DEV), 'dev_sha256': dev_sha, 'dev_rows': len(dev['items']),
 443             'test_path': a.test, 'test_sha256_pinned': TEST_SHA256, 'code_sha256': sha(Path(__file__).read_bytes()),
 444             'b1_sha256': sha(Path(b1.__file__).read_bytes()), 'b0_sha256': sha(Path(b0.__file__).read_bytes()),
 445             'git_head': subprocess.run(['git', '-C', str(HERE), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], capture_output=True,
 446                                        text=True).stdout.strip(),
 447             'command': mem1.command(CTX), 'decode': {**b0.DECODE, **b0.PICK, 'cache_prompt': True}, 'reason': REASON,
 448             'system': SYSTEM, 'dev_items': dev['items'], 'timings': 'NOT MEASUREMENTS'}
 449      (out / 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=1) + '\n')
 450      with open(out / 'requests.jsonl', 'x') as rq, open(out / 'server.log', 'x') as log:
 451          def emit(row):
 452              rq.write(json.dumps(row) + '\n')
 453              rq.flush()
 454          killed, rows, info, proc, exc = False, [], {}, None, None
 455          try:
 456              proc = subprocess.Popen(mem1.command(CTX), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
 457                                      start_new_session=True)
 458              t0 = time.monotonic()
 459              while not cr.healthy():
 460                  if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() - t0 > b1.LOAD_TIMEOUT_S:
 461                      raise RuntimeError(f'llama-server not healthy (exit {proc.poll()})')
 462                  time.sleep(0.1)
 463              run['server_pid'] = proc.pid
 464              rows, info = run_all(b1.Server(), dev, a.test, out, proc.pid, emit)
 465          except BaseException as e:  # recorded; the report and manifest are still written, then a stop re-raises
 466              exc = e
 467              run['exception'] = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
 468          finally:
 469              if proc is not None and proc.poll() is None:
 470                  os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
 471                  try:
 472                      proc.wait(timeout=b1.TERM_WAIT_S)
 473                  except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
 474                      os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
 475                      proc.wait()
 476                      killed = True
 477              run.update(exit_code=proc.returncode if proc else None, killed=killed,
 478                         dev_sha256_end=sha(DEV.read_bytes()))
 479              if info.get('test'):
 480                  run.update(test_sha256=info['test_sha256'], test_rows=len(info['test']['items']),
 481                             test_items=info['test']['items'], test_sha256_end=sha(Path(a.test).read_bytes()))
 482      print(finish(out, run, rows, info) + f'\n{out}')
 483      if exc is not None and not isinstance(exc, Exception):
 484          raise exc
 485  
 486  
 487  if __name__ == '__main__':
 488      main()
END
BEGIN benchmark/mind_picker/b2_rules/test_b2.py (221 lines)
   1  """Offline tests for b2.py with a fake server: rendering of the three variants, two-step V3 scoring, the selection rule
   2  (tie and FLIPS), the TEST lock, symlink custody. No server, no phone. Synthetic sets (the real TEST stays outside the
   3  repo)."""
   4  import json
   5  import os
   6  import re
   7  import sys
   8  import tempfile
   9  import unittest
  10  from pathlib import Path
  11  
  12  sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
  13  sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
  14  import b2  # noqa: E402
  15  import test_b0  # noqa: E402  B0's word-level fake server (b0_cache is on the path via b1)
  16  
  17  N = b2.NAMES
  18  MISSIONS = {'M1': 'Explore and map the flat.', 'M2': 'Find people and keep them company.'}
  19  DEV = {'missions': MISSIONS, 'items': [
  20      {'id': 1, 'mission': 'M1', 'current': 'explore', 'event': 'Someone said: "Stay right there."', 'memory': None,
  21       'allowed': ['explore', 'visit', 'greet', 'look', 'wait'], 'strict': 'wait', 'also_ok': []},
  22      {'id': 2, 'mission': 'M2', 'current': 'visit', 'event': 'A person is close.', 'memory': None,
  23       'allowed': ['explore', 'visit', 'look', 'wait'], 'strict': 'visit', 'also_ok': ['wait']},
  24      {'id': 3, 'mission': 'M2', 'current': 'explore', 'event': 'A person is lying on the couch, close.',
  25       'memory': 'Leo sleeps on the couch; do not wake him.', 'allowed': ['explore', 'visit', 'greet', 'look', 'wait'],
  26       'strict': 'explore', 'also_ok': ['look']}]}
  27  TEST = {'missions': MISSIONS, 'items': [
  28      {'id': 1, 'mission': 'M1', 'current': 'look', 'event': 'You finished looking around the hallway.', 'memory': None,
  29       'allowed': N, 'strict': 'explore', 'also_ok': []},
  30      {'id': 2, 'mission': 'M1', 'current': 'explore', 'event': 'Someone said: "Robot, over here!"', 'memory': None,
  31       'allowed': N, 'strict': 'approach', 'also_ok': []}]}
  32  
  33  
  34  class Fake(test_b0.Fake):
  35      """Tokens keep their leading space (' explore'); /completion answers a reason (n_predict 48) or the favourite
  36      action chosen by `pick(user_text, offered)`; names not offered get the highest raw probability."""
  37  
  38      def __init__(self, pick, alias=None):
  39          super().__init__()
  40          self.pick, self.alias, self.bodies = pick, alias or {}, []
  41  
  42      def ids(self, text):
  43          return [self.vocab.setdefault(self.alias.get(w, w), len(self.vocab) + 10)
  44                  for w in re.findall(r'<\|turn>|<turn\|>| ?\w+|\s|[^\w\s]', text)]
  45  
  46      def text(self, ids):
  47          inv = {v: k for k, v in self.vocab.items()}
  48          return ''.join(inv.get(i, '') for i in ids)
  49  
  50      def __call__(self, path, body=None):
  51          if path == '/health':
  52              return {'status': 'ok'}
  53          if path == '/detokenize':
  54              return {'content': self.text(body['tokens'])}
  55          if path != '/completion':
  56              return super().__call__(path, body)
  57          self.bodies.append(body)
  58          text = self.text(body['prompt'])
  59          if body['n_predict'] == 48:
  60              return {'content': 'Rule 1 fits', 'tokens_predicted': 3, 'stop_type': 'word',
  61                      'timings': {'prompt_n': 5, 'cache_n': 0}}
  62          offered = re.search(r'one word: (.*)\.<turn\|>', text).group(1).replace(' or ', ', ').split(', ')
  63          fav = self.pick(text.split('<|turn>user')[1], offered)
  64          sp = ' ' if text.endswith('Action:') else ''
  65          p = {self.vocab.setdefault(sp + k, len(self.vocab) + 10): 0.5 if k not in offered else 0.4 if k == fav else 0.05
  66               for k in N}
  67          return {'content': fav, 'timings': {'prompt_n': 3, 'cache_n': len(body['prompt']) - 3},
  68                  'completion_probabilities': [{'top_logprobs': [{'id': i, 'prob': v} for i, v in p.items()]}]}
  69  
  70  
  71  def by_label(user, offered):  # the strict label of the item whose event is in the user turn
  72      items = DEV['items'] + TEST['items']
  73      lab = next(it['strict'] for it in items if it['event'] in user)
  74      return {'visit': 'approach'}.get(lab, lab)
  75  
  76  
  77  def first(user, offered):
  78      return offered[0]
  79  
  80  
  81  def sets():
  82      d = json.loads(json.dumps(DEV))
  83      for it in d['items']:  # load_set's renaming, without a file
  84          for k in ('current', 'strict'):
  85              it[k] = {'visit': 'approach'}.get(it[k], it[k])
  86          it['allowed'] = [{'visit': 'approach'}.get(n, n) for n in it['allowed']]
  87          it['also_ok'] = [{'visit': 'approach'}.get(n, n) for n in it['also_ok']]
  88      return d
  89  
  90  
  91  def run(pick, alias=None):
  92      b2.PAUSE_S = 0
  93      fake, events = Fake(pick, alias), []
  94      d = tempfile.mkdtemp()
  95      tp = Path(d) / 'test.json'
  96      tp.write_text(json.dumps(TEST))
  97      b2.TEST_SHA256 = b2.sha(tp.read_bytes())
  98      rows, info = b2.run_all(b2.b1.Server(fake), sets(), tp, Path(d), 7, events.append)
  99      return fake, rows, info, events, Path(d)
 100  
 101  
 102  class T(unittest.TestCase):
 103      def test_rendering(self):
 104          s = sets()
 105          self.assertEqual(s['items'][1]['allowed'], ['explore', 'approach', 'look', 'wait'])
 106          st = b2.state(s, s['items'][1])
 107          self.assertEqual(st, 'Mission: Find people and keep them company.\nCurrent activity: approaching\n'
 108                               'What just happened: A person is close.\nMemory: none\n'
 109                               'Allowed actions: explore, approach, look, wait')
 110          m1, m2, m3 = (b2.messages(s, s['items'][1], v, 'REV') for v in b2.VARIANTS)
 111          self.assertEqual(m1[0]['content'], b2.SYS_RULES)
 112          self.assertTrue(b2.SYS_RULES.endswith('13. Otherwise: keep the current activity.'))
 113          self.assertEqual(m2[0]['content'], b2.SYS_RULES + '\n\nExamples:\n' + '\n'.join(b2.EXAMPLES))
 114          self.assertEqual((len(b2.EXAMPLES), m3[0]), (13, m2[0]))
 115          self.assertEqual(m1[1]['content'], st + '\n\nWhich action next? Answer with one word: wait, look, approach '
 116                                                  'or explore.')
 117          self.assertEqual(m2[1], m1[1])
 118          self.assertEqual(m3[1]['content'], st + "\n\nWhich action next? First write one short sentence naming the rule"
 119                           " that fits. Then write a new line starting with 'Action:' and one word: wait, look, "
 120                           "approach or explore.")
 121          fwd = b2.messages(s, s['items'][0], 'V1', 'FWD')[1]['content']
 122          self.assertTrue(fwd.endswith('one word: explore, approach, greet, look or wait.'))
 123  
 124      def test_v3_two_steps(self):
 125          fake, rows, info, _, _ = run(by_label)
 126          self.assertNotIn('stopped', info)
 127          v3 = [r for r in rows if r['variant'] == 'V3' and r['set'] == 'DEV']
 128          self.assertEqual(len(v3), 6)
 129          k = rows.index(v3[0])
 130          r1, r2 = fake.bodies[12], fake.bodies[13]  # V1 and V2 DEV blocks: 12 single requests, then V3 pairs
 131          self.assertEqual((r1['n_predict'], r1['stop'], r1['cache_prompt']), (48, ['\n'], True))
 132          self.assertEqual(r2['n_predict'], 1)
 133          self.assertEqual(fake.text(r2['prompt']), fake.text(r1['prompt']) + 'Rule 1 fits\nAction:')
 134          self.assertEqual(rows[k]['reason'], 'Rule 1 fits')
 135          self.assertEqual(info['name_ids']['V3'], {n: fake.vocab[' ' + n] for n in N})
 136          self.assertEqual(info['name_ids']['V1/V2'], {n: fake.vocab[n] for n in N})
 137          for r in rows:
 138              it = next(it for it in (DEV if r['set'] == 'DEV' else TEST)['items'] if it['id'] == r['id'])
 139              self.assertAlmostEqual(sum(r['probs'].values()), 1.0)
 140              self.assertEqual((list(r['probs']), r['top']), (r['offered'], by_label(it['event'], None)))
 141  
 142      def test_full_run_and_report(self):
 143          fake, rows, info, events, d = run(by_label)
 144          self.assertEqual(len(rows), 6 * 3 + 2 * 2)
 145          sel = json.loads((d / 'selection.json').read_text())
 146          self.assertEqual((sel['variant'], sel['flag']), ('V1', None))  # all 1.0: tie -> V1
 147          k = next(i for i, e in enumerate(events) if e.get('event') == 'selection')
 148          self.assertTrue(all(e['set'] == 'DEV' for e in events[:k]) and all(e['set'] == 'TEST' for e in events[k + 1:]))
 149          self.assertEqual({(e['variant'], e['order']) for e in events[k + 1:]}, {('V1', 'FWD'), ('V1', 'REV')})
 150          run_rec = {'utc': 'T', 'code_sha256': 'c' * 64, 'git_head': 'h', 'dev_path': '/d', 'dev_sha256': 'd' * 64,
 151                     'dev_sha256_end': 'd' * 64, 'dev_rows': 3, 'dev_items': sets()['items'], 'test_path': '/t',
 152                     'test_sha256': 't' * 64, 'test_sha256_end': 't' * 64, 'test_rows': 2, 'command': ['x'],
 153                     'exit_code': 0, 'killed': False}
 154          text = b2.finish(d, dict(run_rec), rows, info)
 155          self.assertIn('ACCURACY VALID', text)
 156          self.assertIn('-> PASS', text)
 157          self.assertIn("'Rule 1 fits'", text)
 158          text = b2.finish(d, {**run_rec, 'test_sha256_end': 'z'}, rows[:-1], info)
 159          self.assertIn('ACCURACY INVALID: 21/22 rows', text)
 160          self.assertIn('NO RESULT', text)
 161  
 162      def test_selection(self):
 163          def dev(acc, flips):  # acc[v] = (FWD acceptable, REV acceptable)
 164              return {v: ({'strict': 0, 'acceptable': acc[v][0]}, {'strict': 0, 'acceptable': acc[v][1]},
 165                          {'flips': list(range(flips[v]))}) for v in b2.VARIANTS}
 166          sel = b2.select(dev({'V1': (.5, .6), 'V2': (.7, .7), 'V3': (.9, .9)}, {'V1': 0, 'V2': 4, 'V3': 5}))
 167          self.assertEqual((sel['variant'], sel['flag']), ('V2', None))  # V3 excluded by flips
 168          sel = b2.select(dev({'V1': (.5, .6), 'V2': (.6, .5), 'V3': (.55, .55)}, {'V1': 1, 'V2': 0, 'V3': 0}))
 169          self.assertEqual(sel['variant'], 'V1')  # three-way tie on the mean -> V1
 170          sel = b2.select(dev({'V1': (.5, .6), 'V2': (.7, .7), 'V3': (.7, .7)}, {'V1': 9, 'V2': 0, 'V3': 0}))
 171          self.assertEqual(sel['variant'], 'V2')  # tie V2/V3 -> V2
 172          sel = b2.select(dev({'V1': (.5, .6), 'V2': (.7, .7), 'V3': (.8, .7)}, {'V1': 5, 'V2': 6, 'V3': 9}))
 173          self.assertEqual((sel['variant'], sel['flag']), ('V3', 'FLIPS'))
 174          self.assertEqual(sel['dev']['V3']['mean_acceptable'], .75)
 175  
 176      def test_flips_in_run(self):
 177          _, rows, info, _, _ = run(first)  # FWD -> first catalogue name, REV -> wait: every variant flips 3/3
 178          self.assertEqual(info['selection']['flag'], None)  # 3 <= 4
 179          self.assertEqual(info['selection']['dev']['V1']['flips'], [1, 2, 3])
 180  
 181      def test_test_lock(self):
 182          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 183              d, tp = Path(d), Path(d) / 'test.json'
 184              tp.write_text(json.dumps(TEST))
 185              b2.TEST_SHA256 = b2.sha(tp.read_bytes())
 186              with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, 'locked'):
 187                  b2.load_test(d, None, tp)
 188              (d / 'selection.json').write_text('{}')
 189              with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, 'locked'):
 190                  b2.load_test(d, 'f' * 64, tp)  # logged hash differs
 191              self.assertEqual(b2.load_test(d, b2.sha(b'{}'), tp)[0], TEST)
 192              b2.TEST_SHA256 = '0' * 64
 193              with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, 'pinned'):
 194                  b2.load_test(d, b2.sha(b'{}'), tp)
 195  
 196      def test_dev_stop_never_opens_test(self):
 197          fake, rows, info, events, d = run(by_label, alias={'look': 'wait'})  # look and wait share a first token
 198          self.assertIn('STOP before any inference', info['stopped'])
 199          self.assertEqual((rows, fake.bodies, events, 'test' in info), ([], [], [], False))
 200          self.assertFalse((d / 'selection.json').exists())
 201  
 202      def test_custody_symlink(self):
 203          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
 204              link, dlink = Path(d) / 'set.json', Path(d) / 'repo'
 205              os.symlink(b2.DEV, link)  # outside symlink to a file inside the repo
 206              os.symlink(b2.REPO / 'benchmark', dlink)  # outside symlink to a directory inside the repo
 207              self.assertTrue(b2.inside_repo(link))
 208              self.assertTrue(b2.inside_repo(dlink / 'nonexistent.json'))
 209              with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, 'inside the repository'):
 210                  b2.load_set(link, None)
 211              real = Path(d) / 'real.json'
 212              real.write_text('{"items": []}')
 213              self.assertFalse(b2.inside_repo(real))
 214          self.assertTrue(b2.inside_repo(b2.HERE / 'x.json'))
 215          bind = Path('/termux-home/robot')  # the same repo through the proot bind mount: inode check
 216          if bind.exists():
 217              self.assertTrue(b2.inside_repo(bind / 'benchmark' / 'x.json'))
 218

 220  if __name__ == '__main__':
 221      unittest.main()
